公司簡介 
高階機構件滑軌領導品牌製造商 ， 產品應用
在伺服器及廚具 。 隨著近年伺服器市場蓬勃
發展 ， 伺服器導軌已經成為川湖的最核心產
品應用 ， 營收比重高達約9 成 。 川湖具備高
客製化研發能力，以及 3,752 項的專利護
城河 ， 有利為客戶整合開發設計最新且快速
迭代的伺服器規格，推算 AI 伺服器供應市
占率達 80%~90%以上。

主要客戶：伺服器品牌商及美系大型 CSP 
主要競爭對手：

黃璽銘 hsiming@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

川湖 (2059 TT) 
大幅優於預期

永豐觀點 
導軌設計複雜度提高，領先設計開發，高市佔率供應，價值體現於營收獲利成長。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持川湖買進建議：川湖 2Q26 財報獲利及 7 月營收大幅優於預期。川湖
維持 AI 伺服器導軌領導地位，隨著客戶不斷升級AI 伺服器機櫃硬體架構產品，設
計複雜度提高 ， 導軌產品價值提升幅度優於預估 ， 且高市佔率受惠資本支出投入AI
伺服器擴張的趨勢，強勁動能延續至 2027 年後。公司新產能的開出，自動化比例
繼續提高，在拓展營收規模的同時，將能維持獲利率水準。預估2026 年/2027 年
稅後 EPS 327.67元/445.79 元 。 維持買進建議 ， 上修目標價至13,375 元(30 x2027F 
EPS)。

ESG 評析 
川湖企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學)

### Page 2

2

高階機構件滑軌領導品牌製造商：川湖產品應用在伺服器及廚具。 隨著近年伺服器
市場蓬勃發展 ， 伺服器導軌已經成為川湖的最核心產品應用 ， 營收比重高達約9 成。
川湖具備高客製化研發能力 ， 以及3,752 項的專利護城河 ， 有利為客戶整合開發設
計最新且快速迭代的伺服器規格 。川湖與各大伺服器客戶合作 ，囊括伺服器品牌商
及美系大型 CSP，推算 AI 伺服器供應市占率達 80%~90%以上。 
2023 年以前川湖伺服器產品中，以通用伺服器導軌為主，隨著 AI 伺服器需求爆
發 ， 對伺服器機櫃的承重及整體效能要求大幅提升， 帶動高階滑軌規格升級及強勁
需求 。 同時帶動川湖毛利率上升至7 成以上 ， 成為台灣 AI 伺服器供應鏈高獲利的
利基型大廠。依產品組合區分，2Q26 年營收比重：導軌 99.3%、滑軌 0.02%、鉸
鏈 0.2%、其他 0.5%。 
2Q26 財報大幅優於預期：川湖 2Q26 稅後 EPS 74.38 元，毛利率87.4%、營益率
82.1%，皆大幅優於預期，受惠 AI 伺服器輝達 GB300、Blackwell HGX 平台及
AWS Trainium 3 量產規模放大，新平台提高導軌產品的價值較預期高，公司自動
化生產效益顯著提升，毛利率及營益率進一步躍升至8 成以上。2Q26 未分配盈餘
稅約 2.5 億，稅後淨利 70.9 億元，季增 103%，年增 1,054%。 
2H26 營收動能更強勁：7 月營收 64 億元，月增 44%，年增 356%，營收達成率
66%，同樣大幅優於預期。推算包含GB300、B300 HGX、Trainium3、TPU 等 AI
伺服器導軌產品價值較原預期高，且客戶增加訂單，產品放量後帶動營收強勁成
長。預期9 月開始出貨 Vera Rubin 導軌產品，將延續營收動能。上修預估3Q26、
4Q26 營收至 161 億、162 億，毛利率及營益率分別維持在約 85%、80%，稅後
EPS 107.41 元、109.30 元。 
維持 AI 伺服器導軌領導地位 產品價值顯現：先前雖然 AI 伺服器導軌市場同業競
爭市占傳聞不斷，但以川湖財報成績推算，川湖目前在 GB、HGX 系列 AI 伺服器
與 ASIC AI 伺服器導軌市場供應佔有高達 80%以上甚至 90%以上的市佔率，其最
大的護城河來自於強大的客製化研發能力與深厚的專利佈局 。 隨著客戶不斷升級AI
伺服器機櫃硬體架構，機櫃導入液冷散熱設計、產品重量提高，川湖配合客戶開發
導軌需要的新作動功能、增加機構件、提升負荷強度、配合有限空間安裝，整體設
計複雜度提升，公司的客製化設計能力反應在產品價值上揚。且公司成本、費用控
制得宜，營收規模放大的同時，進一步帶動獲利率擴張。 
高市佔率受惠資本支出投入 AI 伺服器上升趨勢：美系 CSP、Neo Cloud 持續增加
資本支出(含四大 CSP、Oracle、Coreweave、SpaceX)，目前 Bloomberg 預估
2026 年、2027 年支出將年增 98%、41%，川湖的高市佔率及跟隨客戶優先開發
新品的技術能力，將優先受惠無論是 GPU、ASIC AI 伺服器的建置需求。另外，近
期 CPU 運算推升通用伺服器需求，以及在輝達 Vera Rubin 世代開始量產後，非
運算櫃的電源、儲存、網通、推論晶片等機櫃形式需求將開始增加，預期帶動導軌
另一大成長動能。 
強勁動能延續至 2027 年：2Q26 財報、7 月營收公告大幅優於預期，推算川湖導
軌產品價量提升，將持續於 ASIC、VR200、MI450 等新一代 AI 伺服器機櫃顯現，
下半年營收動能持續看增至 2027 年，且新產能的開出，自動化比例繼續提高，在
拓展營收規模的同時，將能維持獲利率水準。上調2026 年營收至 485 億元，年增
177%，毛利率 84.9%，營益率 78.9%，稅後淨利 312 億元，年增 217%，稅後
EPS 327.67 元。預期 2027 年 VR200、MI450、Trainium 3氣冷水冷、TPUv8 等
AI 伺服器機櫃及非運算櫃的需求續增、以及 CPU 通用伺服器需求成長放大。整體
市場增加川湖導軌需求的產值優於預期，上修預估 2027 年營收 656 億元，年增
35%，毛利率 85.2%，營益率 80.1%，稅後淨利 425 億元，稅後 EPS 445.79 元。

營運現況與分析

## Document 072: 2382廣達 元富

Source PDF: all report pdf/2382廣達 元富.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-17
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為元富投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

廣達(2382) 
建議：維持買進 
結論 ：毛利率 、 營益率雖承壓 ， 但營運及獲利成長無虞，
且 PER 偏低

一、2Q26 毛利率 、 營益率優於預期 ， 本業達成率102%，
業外淨收入遠優於預期，獲利達成率 111%，單季稅後
EPS 7.11 元，累計 1H26 稅後 EPS 12.36 元 
廣達公告 2Q26 營收 1.036 兆元 、+28%QoQ、+106%YoY，
Server 及 NB 兩大產品線均強勁季成長雙位數，其中
Server 成長高於 NB，毛利率 5.02%(原 5%)、
+0.24pptsQoQ、-2.03pptsYoY，毛利率在 1Q26 新舊品
交替 ASP 變動顯著影響，2Q26 影響已和緩，Server 機
櫃 ASP 大致持平，但仍受到 Memory、CPU 及其他材
料成本上升影響，營業利益 342 億元、+48%QoQ、
+68%YoY，達成率 102%，略優於預期，其中費用率持
續創新低來到 1.72%、-0.22pptsQoQ，主要受惠營收規
模快速擴大帶來營運槓桿，營益率 3.3%(原 3.25%)、
+0.45pptsQoQ、-0.75pptsYoY， 業外淨收入23.17億元，
高於預期，其中匯兌收益高達 32.73 億元、利息淨支出
16.93 億元 ， 稅前淨利365 億元 、+49%QoQ、+69%YoY，
稅後淨利 286 億元、+35%QoQ、+70%YoY，獲利達成
率 111%， 單季稅後EPS 7.11 元(原 6.37 元)。 累計1H26
營收 1.845 兆元、+86%YoY，營業利益 572 億元、
+27%YoY，稅前淨利 610 億元、+30%YoY，稅後淨利
498 億元、+37%YoY，稅後 EPS 12.36 元。

二、Server 占營收比重逾 8 成 ， 其中AI Server 占 Server
比重約 75~80%，ASIC 比重仍低僅占 AI Server 約 10%；
2Q26 NB 季增雙位數，淡季不淡  
Server 占營收比重已穩定超過 80%，1H26 AI Server
占 Server 營收比重約 75~80%，目前前 AI Server 主要
營收來自 Top 5 CSP 客戶，隨著逐步擴展 NeoCloud 及
ASIC 客戶 ， 有利於改善客戶集中度並提高產品多元性，
NeoCloud GPU Server 及 CSP ASIC Server 專案均已
小量出貨，惟 ASIC 目前占 AI Server 比重仍<10%。 
2Q26 NB 營收占營收比重約低雙位數，出貨量約 1,150
萬台、+15%QoQ、-5%YoY，顯著優於公司原先預期的
持平或小幅成長，亦優於過去 2Q 季節性增幅，主要係
因品牌客戶預期 Memory 價格持續上漲，產能受到 AI 
Server 排擠，而提前備貨。雖如此，但 Memory、CPU
等零組件缺貨及價格持續上漲 ， 仍壓抑NB 毛利率表現。 
2Q26 智慧駕駛及電動車相關產品營收均呈雙位數季成
長及年成長，顯示車載產品組合調整大致已告一段落，
終端市場亦逐步回溫，因 AI Server 營收快速擴大，車
載業務營收占比被稀釋至低個位數，但仍持續取得新專
案，並規劃擴充德國智慧駕駛相關產線。 
三、因應未來AI 龐大需求，除持續擴增美國加州及泰國
廠外，今年底產能倍增，擬取得友達華亞廠因應 2028
年後的需求，資本支出由 300 億元上修至 400 億元 
2Q26 資本支出 136 億元，累計 1H26 資本支出 218 億
元，主要用以美國加州廠及泰國廠的廠房設備擴充，占
資本支出比重約 8 成 ， 公司持續進行AI 相關產能擴充，
2026 年年底達產能倍增的目標(相對 2025 年底)。 同時，
廣達擬以 197 億元取得友達光電的華亞廠，該廠預計
4Q27 完成點交 ， 用以支應2028 年的 Server 訂單需求，
該廠具備較高的電力容量配置，對高階 AI Server 快速
擴充產能具有關鍵優勢，該廠加入後，有利於 2028 年
台灣 AI Server 後段組裝產能倍增，亦因此廣達調整
2026 年資本支出由 300 億元上修至 400 億元。 
四、3Q26 成長動能仍來自 AI Server 與 General，NB
出貨則季哀退 2 成以上，惟毛利率仍承壓，力拼營益率
持穩 1H26 水準 ，推估營收季增 10%， 單季稅後EPS 7.54
元 
展望 3Q26，NB 需求展望保守，零組件成本上升迫使品
牌客戶調高 NB 售價，進一步抑制消費者需求，預期
3Q26 出貨量將季衰退逾 2 成；Server 中，AI Server 營
收持續維持強勁的雙位數季成長 ， 但因基期已明顯提高 ，
其成長幅度將低於 2Q26，General Server 亦維持強勁
成長，且成長率高於GPU Server，惟 GPU Server 單價

比重偏低，毛利率仍會受產品組合影響，但營益率則有

收 3,662 億元、-5%MoM、+131%YoY，季營收達成率
32%，累計營收+93%YoY。合併毛利率 5%、
-0.02pptsQoQ、-1.85pptsYoY，合併營益率 3.4%、
+0.1pptsQoQ、-0.31pptsYoY，稅後淨利 304 億元、
+6%QoQ、+85%YoY，稅後 EPS 7.54 元(原 6.26 元)。 
五、展望 2026 年，AI Server 維持三位數成長，通用
Server 維持雙位數成長，NB 出貨量雙位數衰退，上修
2026 年營收至 YoY 96%，稅後 EPS 27.79 元 
展望 2026 年，NB 出貨量維持先前與產業平均相當的看
法，記憶體短缺帶動成本與售價上揚，削弱使用者採購
意願，使市場能見度偏低，客戶規劃轉趨保守，2026 年
出貨量將有雙位數年衰退；Server(包括 General Server
及 AI Server)能見度很高，客戶已開始討論 2027~2028
年的產能、電力規劃及電力需求解決方案，公司高度樂
觀看待 2027 及 2028 年需求，部分客戶已提供延伸至
2028 年的需求規劃。其中 General Server 全年展望維
持雙位數成長目標，AI Server 仍是主要成長動能，維持
年成長三位數百分比 ，1H26 AI Server 占 Server 比重約
75~80%，2H26 比重仍將進一步提高，預期 2026 年 AI 
Server 占 Server 營收 80%的目標不變。整體Server 自
1Q26 起已穩定占合併營收的 80%以上 ，NB 已降低至低
雙位數營收比重，此趨勢仍將持續。營益率方面，公司
預期營益率目標能維持 1H26 3.1%的水準，2H26 AI 
Server 占比提升有助產品組合，但 NB 出貨下滑將不利
經濟規模，兩者間將相互抵銷。投顧預估 2026 年營收
4.168 兆元(原 3.915 兆元)、+96%YoY， 毛利率4.96%、
-2.02pptsYoY，營益率 3.28%、-0.84pptsYoY，稅後淨
利 1,120 億元、YoY49%，稅後 EPS 27.79 元(原 24.5
元)。同時預估 2027 年營收 5,77 兆元、+38%YoY，毛
利率 4.67%、-0.29pptsYoY， 營益率3.2%、-0.07pptsYoY，
稅後淨利 1,446 億元 、+29%YoY， 稅後EPS 35.87 元。 
六、維持買進理由 
投顧預估 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 27.79 元及
35.87 元，目前股價325 元，PER <12X 及 9X 不高，短
期毛利率雖仍承壓、但營益率已回穩，隨 VR 2H26 及
1H27 出貨，毛利率、營益率仍舊承壓，惟營益金額仍能
顯著年成長 ， 故PER 仍難回到過去榮景 ， 隨營收環境的
成長無虞，股價基期偏低下，仍有利走勢，故維持買進
投資評等，目標價維持 420 元(PER 15X 2026EPS)。

基本資料 結論與建議

5.0%(季增 0.2/年減 2.0ppts)，優於預期 0.4ppts；營業淨

較高基期，Q3 季增幅度將較 Q2 收斂。預期隨著 ASIC、
NV MGX ETL等新平台產品於26Q4~2027年進入量產階
段，伺服器市場需求能見度已看至 2028 年，廣達亦能掌
握一定程度的組裝市場份額。

營 運 分 析

廣達為全球最大的 Notebook ODM廠 
廣達電腦股份有限公司成立於 1988 年，總部位於台灣桃園，於台灣、中國(上
海、重慶)、美國(加州)、泰國、越南、墨西哥、德國皆有設立研發/製造中心，
主要經營 Notebook(消費與商用筆電)、Cloud Server(通用型伺服器、存儲與
網通設備)、AI Server(NV 與 AMD等系統平台)、車用電子、醫療與機器人等
硬體產品的設計、製造及服務及售後服務。廣達現為全球最大的 Notebook 
ODM 廠(全球市占率 30%)，並於近年積極於 AI 伺服器系統級組裝之份額中
佔有一席之地，AI 伺服器占整體伺服器營收比例已達 75~85%，公司客戶結
構亦逐步由既有大型 CSP 延伸至 Neocloud與 ASIC 客戶，廣達 AI 伺服器客
戶與平台組合正逐步多元化，有助降低單一客戶或單一 GPU 平台世代轉換
造成的營運波動。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q2毛利與淨利率優於預期 
廣達 26Q2 營收 1.0 兆元(QoQ+28.1%/YoY+105.6%)，伺服器與筆電營收皆
呈雙位數季增，其中伺服器成長幅度優於筆電，主要受惠高階 AI 伺服器出
貨量快速增加。26Q2 AI Server 系統機櫃平均出貨價格與前季大致相當，預
估 26Q2廣達 GB300約出貨 6.5~7千櫃(26Q1約 4~4.5千櫃) ，因此本季 AI 
伺服器營收大幅季增主要來自實際出貨量增加。 
毛利率 5.0%(季增 0.2/年減 2.0ppts)，優於預期 0.4ppts，高階 AI 伺服器量
產出貨的稀釋效應於 26Q1 一次拉高後趨於和緩；營業淨利 342.4 億元
(QoQ+48.7%/YoY+67.8%)，因毛利率高於預期而優於預期 9.4%，稅後淨利
286.5 億元(QoQ+35.2%/YoY+69.9%)，優於預期 18.1%，主因業外收益高於
預期與稅率低於預期，EPS 7.43 元，優於預期的 6.28 元。

26Q2 筆電出貨量優於預期，惟 26H2 出貨量將因記憶體成本高漲而衰退  
26Q2 廣達筆電出貨量 1,150 萬台(QoQ+15%/ YoY-5%)，優於兆豐預期
11.6%及公司前季展望預期(原估持平~小幅季增)，主因新機種上市動能延續
至 26Q2，與客戶預期記憶體價格將持續高居不下，提前啟動拉貨下半年的
拉貨。而 Q2 為傳統 Chromebook 旺季，Chromebook 出貨僅占 26Q2 筆電
出貨的不到 10%，顯示低單價筆電產品受記憶體價格影響，成本壓力大進而
使出貨量大降。預估進入 26Q3 筆電出貨量將季減 20%與年減雙位數比率。

26H2 AI 伺服器維持成長，GPU、ASIC與通用型需求同步推進 
展望 26H2，預期 AI 伺服器營收仍將逐季成長，惟因 26Q2 較高基期，Q3
季增幅度將較 Q2收斂， 預估廣達26Q3/Q4 GB300將出貨約 5.8~6.0/4.5~4.7
千櫃 ， 另外預估26Q4將出貨 VR約 0.8~1.0千櫃與些微的 AMD Helios機櫃 。 
近期從供應鏈觀察到 Neocloud客戶對於新舊平台的 GPU伺服器需求皆顯著
增加，也預估考量新平台維護成本等因素， GB300 生命周期將延長。目前
GPU 伺服器仍為 AI 伺服器營收主要來源，但公司也持續拓展 ASIC 與
Neocloud 客戶。26H1 ASIC 專案占 AI Server 營收比例仍低於 10%，預期
隨著 ASIC、NV MGX ETL 等新平台產品於 26Q4~2027 年進入量產階段，
廣達亦能掌握一定程度的組裝市場份額。而通用型伺服器 2026 年出貨量成
長率甚至有機會高於 GPU 伺服器，主要受惠 CPU供應情況改善與一般伺服
器需求回升，整體伺服器市場需求能見度已看至 2028 年。

預估廣達 26Q3 營收 1.06 兆元(QoQ+2.2%/YoY+113.9%)；毛利率 5.1%(季
持平/年減 1.8ppts)；營業淨利 354.4 億元(QoQ+3.5%/YoY+92.9%)；稅後淨
利 273.4 億元(QoQ-4.6%/YoY+66.4%)，EPS 7.08 元。

### Page 3

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
11 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Micro-Star
Valuation
Our target price of NT$118 is based on c.12x 2026E EPS, below the stock's five-year average forward 12-month PE. This
valuation reflects the earnings downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring component costs,
while accounting for the time required for the server business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in

high-end gaming demand; and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server segment. Key downside
risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs
due to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

GCE’s 2Q26 core business (OPI) was 6%/2% lower than GSe/BBG 
consensus, mainly due to the less favorable than expected GM (was 
2.2%/1.4ppt below GSe/BBG consensus) resulting from the 
mismatch on component cost hike vs. GCE’s product pricing hike 
and the increasing ramp up cost in its new Thailand plant (the 
cost-pricing mismatch and high ramping up cost will both be 
solved in 3Q26). However, the company’s net income was 3/8% 
higher than GSe/BBG consensus, thanks to the more favorable tax 
rate (24% vs. GSe/BBG consensus at 32%/32%) driven mainly by its 
increasing earnings contribution from its new Thailand plant 
(Thailand plant tax rate is significantly lower than Taiwan and 
mainland China plants).  
For 3Q26, we expect the company’s revenue to grow by 26% QoQ 
(in-line with 2Q revenue growth) with (1) the 12% more capacity 
value in 3Q vs. 2Q, (2) the increasing contribution from high ASP 
ASIC products, and (3) the much better pricing in 3Q vs. 2Q (we 
expect the company’s overall product pricing to go up by 10-15%+ 
in 3Q). In terms of GM/OPM, we expect the company’s GM/OPM 
to increase to 37.9%/30.3% (up 3.2ppt/3.9ppt QoQ) thanks to 
the much better product mix and pricing condition for its PCB 
products.  
Investment view - GCE continues to be the most competitive AI 
ASIC PCB supplier, with easing yield rate risk on ASIC HDI 
We continue to expect GCE will hold the major share for Trainium 3 
(70%+ from 3Q26) with its smooth ramp up in its new Thailand 
capacity, which we believe will start to gain share in the TPU supply 
chain from the end of 2026 and should see significant improvement 
on the market share (<5% in 2026 to 20-30%+ in 2027), leading to a 
fast capacity expansion speed in coming years (we believe capacity 
volume will grow by ~30% 2025-28E CAGR vs. 7% 2021-25 CAGR). 
Based on our calculation, GCE could hold 15-17% of global AI PCB

### Page 3

market share in 2026-28E while its ASIC PCB market share should be consistently higher 
than 25% from 2026 (to 30-32% in 2027-28E), suggesting not only a solid revenue 
growth outlook, but also imply better profitability in the long term (we believe AI grade 
PCB GM is and will continue to be 45-50%+ vs. GCE 2Q GM at only at ~35%).  
Moreover, the company mentioned its progress on ramping up thick-copper HDI (for AI 
server) capacity is smooth now and management believe the difficulty on producing AI 
HDI products should be similar to 800G switches (we believe the yield rate should be at 
least 70-80%), and we believe the positive comment on AI HDI yield rate suggests more 
opportunity for the company to enjoy an even better market share and profitability in 
coming quarters/years.  
We suggest investors to build position on GCE after a significant de-rating on P/E 
multiple, before the yield rate ramp up on its new AI ASIC MLB/RPCB capacity, as we 
have already seen the company’s stock price de-rate by 5-10x (was 20-25x in 2024-25, 
but the company only traded at 10-11x of 2027E EPS based on our estimate) due to (1) 
investors’ concern on GCE’s AI HDI yield rate (which could impact the company’s market 
share and profitability in the LT), and (2) the relatively slow GM expansion speed 
especially in 1H26. We believe investors’ concern on GCE AI HDI yield rate will gradually 
ease in coming quarters after the company ramps up all of its new high-end HDI 
capacity; also, considering the much better pricing condition and product mix in 3Q, we 
believe investors will start to revisit GCE’s business once they realize the 2Q GM miss is 
only a one-time issue driven by the pricing/cost hike timing mismatch. As such, we see 
attractive upside on GCE (87% implied upside vs. today) with limited risk in the short 
term to long term.  
Maintain Buy on GCE, with a new 12-m TP of NT$1,800 (from NT$1,585).

Earning and TP revision

Earnings revision 
We revise up our 2026/27/28E earnings estimate by 9/14/13% to factor in the faster 
than expected ASP hike from high-end AI server, as well as the much faster than 
originally expected capacity expansion plans (revise up 2026/27/28E revenue estimate 
by 3/7/6%), despite our less positive view on the GM outlook (due to the unfavorable FX 
movement).

### Page 4

TP revision 
Maintain Buy with a new TP of NT$1,800 (from NT$1,585), based on the new 20x P/E 
(revise down from 22x, to factor in investors’ concern on GCE’s AI HDI yield rate; 20x P/E 
is in-line with the AI PCB industry average valuation in the past 3 years),  also rollover of 
our valuation base from 4Q26-3Q27E to 2027E, and factoring in our earnings revision.

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

GCE is a key cloud (server+switch) PCB supplier globally with 20%+ market share in 
2022. We expect cloud PCB market demand to grow at a 15% 2024-26E CAGR (far 
stronger than the overall RPCB+HDI market demand growth rate at 5% 2024-26E 
CAGR). We believe GCE’s proactive capacity expansion plan (33% in 2026) and high 
annualized ROI (300%+) will continue to drive the company’s revenue/margin level in the 
coming quarters/years. Additionally, we believe GCE can continue to see strong demand 
from the increasing content per server/switch, as we expect both markets to see a 
20-50%+ layer count increase after the generation upgrade (upgrade to 400G/Eagle 
Stream). We are constructive on GCE’s long-term growth opportunity given its solid 
position in the rapidly growing cloud PCB market, and we are Buy rated on the stock. 
GCE is trading higher than its historical P/E average level driven by strong AI server 
demand, but lower than the TW AI server players’ average.

demand, and (4) higher-than-expected ramp up cost for new capacity

return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 107: 260812_daiwa_lmoc

Source PDF: all report pdf/260812_daiwa_lmoc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: On 12 August, LandMark Opto (LMO) disclosed 2Q26 results 
and 3Q26 guidance both beating market expectations, thanks to cost 
passthrough through the supply chain, on top of the continued strong 
demand for silicon photonics (SiPh) as cloud service providers (CSP) 
actively build out AI datacentres that spur demand for optical bandwidths 
scaling out, up and across. With the 2 growth drivers set to continue, we lift 
our forecasts and TP, but reaffirm our Sell (5) rating on valuation grounds 
as the stock still trades at over 100x 2027E PER and 37x 2027E PBR, all-
time highs in history which don’t seem to be justified by fundamentals, in 
our view.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat but ... LMO released 
2Q26 EPS (after factoring in a 10% stock dividend) of TWD4.6, beating 
our/consensus estimates by 28%/21% thanks to: 1) higher top line 
associated with price hikes on a passthrough of increased materials costs 
such as substrates (see our memo), which added to gross margin, and 2) 
frugal R&D control for opex ratio that finished 2.9pp below our estimate at 
only 10.6%. Guidance calls for 3Q26 revenue QoQ growth >= that of 2Q25 
(22% QoQ), beating our previous/consensus forecasts of up 8%/11% QoQ, 
thanks to: 1) the continued strong demand for SiPh (ie, CW laser) from 
hyperscale CSPs and module makers across the US, China and Taiwan, 
and 2) likely the continued cost passthrough through the supply chain. All in 
all, we lift our 2026-28E EPS by 21-32%.

… execution and valuation still under test. Despite the upbeat results 
and guidance, we remain uncertain about LMO’s margin execution on 
increased depreciation associated with hefty capex, increased headcount 
and variety of SiPh product specs that may affect yields and learning curve 
pickups in the downstream subcontractors. This execution is under scrutiny 
as flagged in our previous note. On top of that, more importantly, valuations 
look overstretched still since, after our forecast revisions, the stock still 
trades at over 100x 2027E PER and 37x 2027E PBR (charts on p.2).

What we recommend: Given our forecast changes, we lift our 12M TP for 
LMO to TWD900 (previous: TWD586), based on our higher ROE-adjusted 
PBR of 13x (previous: 10x) 4-quarter forward BVPS due to a higher ROE 
trajectory. We reaffirm our Sell (5) call on valuation grounds, with upside 
risk of any better-than-expected SiPh ramp and margin execution.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 29-49% below the consensus 
forecasts as we believe the street is too aggressive on the SiPh strength.

12 August 2026

Off to a strong start but … (III)

➢ 2Q26 results and 3Q26 guidance all beat estimates 
➢ Lift forecasts on continued AI strength and cost passthrough 
➢ Reaffirm Sell (5) on valuation despite a higher TP of TWD900

Company profile 
Founded in June 1997, LandMark Optoelectronics Corporation (LMO) is a dedicated compound 
semiconductor epiwafer supplier for optical communication (OC), with end-applications focusing 
primarily on telecom and datacom, including passive optical network (PON), datacentre and cellular 
infrastructure, as well as industrial and consumer electronics. LMO is one of the largest pure OC 
epiwafer suppliers globally by revenue.

## Document 108: 260812_daiwa_lotes

Source PDF: all report pdf/260812_daiwa_lotes.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Lotes released its 2Q26 results after market hours on 12 August. Lotes' 2Q26 
earnings came in at TWD2.0bn (EPS: TWD17.6), which was 30% lower than our and 17% 
lower than the street's expectations. While revenue was slightly lower than expected, 2Q26 
gross margin decreased by 4.0pp QoQ to 45.5%, driven mainly by rising raw material costs 
(eg, copper, gold, plastics) and a delayed margin uplift from new-generation CPU platforms 
due to component shortages in the supply chain. Operating margin declined 7.0pp QoQ to 
22.0%, reflecting higher-than-expected opex from a one-time employee stock bonus 
expense. Looking into 3Q26, we expect top-line momentum to remain robust, driven 
mainly by strength in Lotes' QD business. However, while QD growth supports the top line, 
it also weighs on margins given its lower profitability profile. Combined with elevated raw 
material costs and an unfavourable product mix shift, we believe margin pressure will 
persist into 3Q26. We think a meaningful improvement in profitability will require higher 
penetration of next-generation CPU platforms, which should provide a more substantial 
margin boost once ramped. That said, despite these near-term headwinds, we remain 
positive on Lotes given its long-term positioning as a key CPU server play. Lotes is hosting 
results call on 13 August and we will provide updates in due course. We have a Buy (1) 
rating on Lotes with a 12M TP of TWD3,533, based on a target PER of 28x applied to our 
1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key risk: lower-than-expected adoption of server CPU 
platforms.

Lotes’ 2Q26 results highlights

What’s the impact: 
Below are our read-throughs from Lotes’ 2Q26 results. Lotes is hosting results call on 13 
August and we will provide updates in due course.

• 2Q26 results dragged by gross margin and high opex. Lotes’ 2Q26 earnings came 
in at TWD2.0bn (EPS: TWD17.6), which was 30% lower than our and 17% lower than 
the street’s expectations. While revenue was slightly lower than expected, 2Q26 gross 
margin decreased by 4.0pp QoQ to 45.5%, driven mainly by rising raw material costs 
(eg, copper, gold, plastics) and a delayed margin uplift from new-generation CPU 
platforms due to component shortages in the supply chain. Operating margin declined 
7.0pp QoQ to 22.0%, reflecting higher-than-expected opex from a one-time employee 
stock bonus expense. As for net profit, it was boosted by a higher-than-expected non-op 
gain of c.TWD500m (vs. our estimate of c.TWD120m).

Lotes: monthly revenue trend and YoY growth

Source: Company

• Business outlook. Lotes reported July sales at TWD3.4bn (up 9% MoM and 19% YoY), 
which achieved 29% of our 3Q26 sales forecast (TWD11.7bn; up 23% QoQ and 39% 
YoY) and 31% of the Bloomberg consensus of TWD11.0bn. With July revenue 
accounting for 31-34% of Lotes’s 3Q revenue over the past five years, the run-rate is 
largely on track with the street’s expectation. Looking into 3Q26, we expect top-line 
momentum to remain robust, driven mainly by strength in Lotes' QD business. However, 
while QD growth supports the top line, it also weighs on margins given its lower 
profitability profile. Combined with elevated raw material costs and an unfavourable 
product mix shift, we believe margin pressure will persist into 3Q26. We think a 
meaningful improvement in profitability will require higher penetration of next-generation 
CPU platforms, which should provide a more substantial margin boost once ramped. 
That said, despite these near-term headwinds, we remain positive on Lotes given its 
long-term positioning as a key CPU server play. Lastly, Lotes announced an issuance of 
new shares (dilution: c.1.15%) and a TWD3bn convertible bond issuance (dilution: 
c.1.34%). Lotes is hosting results call on 13 August and we will provide updates in due 
course.

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Lotes with a 12M TP of TWD3,533, based on a target PER of 
28x applied to our 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key risk: lower-than-expected adoption of 
server CPU platforms.

## Document 111: 260812_daiwa_nienmade

Source PDF: all report pdf/260812_daiwa_nienmade.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: Nien Made reported its 2Q26 results on 12 August 2026. We 
remain positive on the stock due to its leading industry position and see a 
higher market-share gain potential on top of its solid execution capability in 
the long term. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our previous flash, Intact 
2026 outlook, 24 June 2026, for more information).

What’s the impact: Record-high gross margin but headwinds caused 
by oil price hike and tax payment in China. Nien Made’s 2Q26 revenue 
was TWD7,928m (+8.5% QoQ, +2.9% YoY), in line with our and the 
consensus estimates. Its gross margin was 60.0%, a record-high, up from 
59.1% in 1Q26 and 59.2% in 2Q25, beating our estimate (59.4%) and the 
consensus estimates (59.5%) on price hikes in its custom-made segment 
with lower tariff. Its operating margin came in at 27.2%, down from 28.1% 
in 1Q26 and 31.1% in 2Q25, lower than our estimate (29.9%) and the 
consensus estimates (30.0%), due to surging fuel surcharges following the 
oil price hike in 2Q26. As such, operating profit was TWD2,160m (+5.2% 
QoQ, -9.8% YoY), missing our and the market consensus estimates by 
7.6% and 9.9%, respectively. With a one-off retrospective tax payment in 
China, 2Q26 net profit stood at TWD1,391m (-15.6% QoQ, +20.7% YoY), 
missing our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates by 18.4% and 23.2%, 
respectively. Its 2Q26 EPS came in at TWD4.75.

Aiming to gain market share. Nien Made maintains its target of growing 
its custom-made revenue in the US by mid-single digit % YoY, custom-
made in non-US markets by high-single digit % YoY, and ready-made 
revenue by low-single digit % YoY in 2026E. Nien Made expects its 
revenue momentum to continue in the following months, considering the 
ramping up of new capacity in northern Vietnam and Mexico. There should 
be some US tax refunds in 2H26. Nien Made will continue to invest in its 
window covering brands, channels and service network as well as 
manufacturing, which should be the key factor to drive the company to win 
more order shares in the long run, in our view. We trim our 2026-27E EPS 
by 2-4%, after factoring in 2Q26 results and more aggressive opex 
assumptions.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call on Nien Made with an 
unchanged 12M TP of TWD515, still based on a target PER of 20x on our 
1-year forward EPS vs. its past 3-year PER trading range of 11-21x. Key 
downside risks: lower-than-expected revenue from custom-made products 
and macro/tariff uncertainties.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 2-6% below the consensus, as we 
are slightly more conservative on its operating margins.

12 August 2026

2Q26 results: record-high gross margin

➢ 2Q26 net profit missed on higher opex and tax  
➢ Revenue momentum should heat up in 2H26 on capacity expansion 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating and unchanged 12M TP of TWD515

Company profile 
Founded in July 2007 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in December 2015, Nien Made Enterprise 
(Nien Made) produces window coverings such as venetian blinds, shutters and shades (ie, cellular 
shades, roller blinds and roman blinds). Its major clients include dealers, designers and small specialty 
stores as well as big box retailers (ie, Home Depot and Wal-Mart).

• GM came in at 45.5% (-410bps q/q, -550bps y/y) 340bps below MSe and
380bps below the Street. This is mainly due to rising raw material costs and
depletion of low cost raw material inventory, with Lotes unable to raise
prices to end customers, until new project price negotiations.
• OPEX is  also higher than expected at NT$2.25B (+18% q/q), 17% above MSe

and 12% above the Street.
• So OpM declined to 22.0% (-710bps q/q, -920bps y/y), 790bps below MSe
and 740bps below the Street.
• Net profit was NT$1,984M (-17% q/q, +165% y/y) for EPS of NT$17.69, 11%
below MSe and 14% below the Street.
• Lotes is holding an earnings call on August 13 at 10am HKT; we will share
takeaways after the call.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lotes Co. Ltd. (3533.TW)
Primary - Residual Income
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.6% (beta of
1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected growth in the server business, such as from cloud and enter-
prise in China
n Stronger-than-expected DT demand

n Sooner-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n Faster ramp-up of its EV business
Risks to Downside
n Further deceleration of server demand from US hyperscalers
n Weaker-than-expected DT demand
n Slower-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n More severe pricing erosion driven by competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Bull/Bear: Lotes  (3533.TW)
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 6

Lotes  (3533.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
6
Lotes
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 2007, Lotes is a leading solution provider of
connectors and cable assembly services based in Keelung, Taiwan. The
company runs a fully integrated business model, with product design, tooling,
molding, stamping, electro-plating and automation process all under the
same roof. Majority of Lotes’ manufacturing capacity is located in China,
Guangzhou, Huanan, and Suzhou, with headquarters located in Keelung,

Taiwan. The main focus areas include CPU socket/ILM and connectors for
DDR, PCIe, SATA, Type-C, and etc. Specifically for CPU socket, Lotes is now
the No.2 supplier globally with 25%+ of global market share. Lotes also owns
3,200+ patents globally for product design and manufacturing process
protection, which has raised the bar for new competition. Lotes started the
business as a PC connector supplier but has shifted its focus to server and
other non-PC business in recent years. Management targets to increase its

non-PC related exposure 70% to total sales in next 5 years, vs 56% in 2018.
Investment strategy
We rate Lotes as Buy. Lotes has c25%+ global market share in CPU sockets
and is seeing rising exposure to servers. We expect Lotes to grow its sales and
earnings at 16% and 27% CAGR in 2023-25E, backed by server sales growth,
CPU platform migration and spec migration in sockets. We forecast server-
related sales to account for 30-35% of total sales in 2024-25E, vs. 28% in

2023. As server products generate better margins than PCs, we expect
margins to improve based on product mix changes and better manufacturing
yield as well as cost efficiency. We also expect CPU platform transition and
spec migration of connectors to lead to ASP increases amid limited
competition.
Valuation
Our target price for Lotes is NT$2,500. We value Lotes based on PE as we
believe the company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be

the key focus of the market. Our target price is based on 20x 2027E EPS,
which is at the higher-end of the historical trading range since 2015 and is
justified by the company’s successful transformation from a PC-centric
component maker to a company with increasing server exposure (posing
higher entry barriers), robust earnings growth and margin expansion, as well
as rising ROE.
Risks
Key downside risks that could cause Lotes shares to trade below our target

price include: 1) Potential delays in CPU launch schedule; 2) Peer competition;
3) Raw material price hike; 4) Patent litigation; and 5) FX volatility.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

institutional clients and the management teams of covered companies. Typically, company management is more likely to
participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 9

Lotes  (3533.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
9
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

Earnings revision: We factor in the 2Q26 earnings and revise up 2026E earnings by 1% 
on higher-than-expected GM and lower-than-expected Opex ratio, while non-OP is 
lower than expected. We keep 2027/ 28E earnings largely unchanged.

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and remains based on a 
P/E. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 18.9x is derived from a peers’ average ratio of 
trading P/E to forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the sum of NI YoY 
and OPM). Our target P/E of 18.9x is in the range of company’s historical forward P/E 
since 2010, reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business expansion from the 
competitive consumer electronics market to AI servers and EVs outsourcing, which 
should drive profit expansion with diversifying business opportunities for Hon Hai. 
Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 134: 260813_ms_compal

Source PDF: all report pdf/260813_ms_compal.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-13
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Update
Compal Electronics  | Asia Pacific
2Q26 GM missed despite non-
PC strength; stay UW
PCs are the near-term drag: Management expects its 3Q NB shipment units to be
flat q/q thanks to share gains; higher ASPs support sequential PC revenue growth.
For the full year, Compal expects its PC shipments to decline ~15% y/y, in line with
the market.
AI servers remain the growth driver: AI servers made up ~70% of server revenue in
2Q (servers' share of total revenue was in mid-single digits in 1Q an d high single

digits in 2Q), with new Neocloud wins widening the pipeline. 3Q server revenue is
guided to grow in high double digits q/q. For full-year 2026, servers are targeted at
~10% of the mix (unchanged). They are guided to rise to more than 30–40% in
2027, potentially taking PCs below 50% of total revenue (though we remain
skeptical).
Compal currently mainly ships L6 for general servers and L10 for AI servers (B300
HGX), both of which carry higher GM and OpM vs. AI racks and the corporate

average., but it expects more rack-scale AI server shipments from late 2026
onwards. This implies that Compal's margins could trend lower h/h because of a
different product mix within servers in 2H. Of note,  Compal's AI server business
currently runs 100% on buy-and-sell.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

Key Takeaways
CY26 capex is guided to be NT$18bn; 1H26 capex was ~NT$9bn, NT$6-7bn of
which was dedicated to server capacity expansion.

CY27 capex is expected to remain elevated, though no numerical guidance was
provided.
2Q GM was 4.6% (-60bps q/q), 80bps below our estimate/70 bps below the
Street. Higher component costs diluted GM.
2Q OP was NT$2.9bn (+8% q/q), 13% below our estimate/9% below the Street,
for OpM of 1.2% (-10bps q/q), reflecting operating leverage.
2Q non-op income was higher because of FVTPL gains. Thus, NP reached NT
$3.1bn (+59% q/q), 44% above our estimate/39% above the Street, for EPS of NT

$0.72.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Compal Electronics (2324.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.1% (beta
1.3, equity risk premium 6.0%, and risk-free rate 1.5%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%,
and a long-term earnings growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB and smartphone demand
n Further share gains in iPad
n Margin expansion thanks to better scale

Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Margin contraction from sales shortfall and fierce price competition
n Weaker-than-expected iPad shipments

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 3

Alchip (3661.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
3
Existing Customer Secured; Next Major CSP Opportunity Emerging  — Alchip
believes its existing CSP relationship is increasingly sticky after multiple
generations of cooperation, with the customer already assigning it the next few
generational designs and architectures. Customer concentration nevertheless
remains high, and management acknowledged that meaningful diversification
requires winning another major hyperscaler, we believe is Google as it is the biggest

AI ASIC customer with large volume.  As CSPs increasingly adopt COT and multi-
vendor sourcing, Alchip expects additional opportunities across compute-die
design, KGD and packaging integration, supporting management’s expectation for
AI-related growth broadly in line with key customers through 2029.
Maintaining Buy, TP NT$6,800 — Given higher ASP , more value added from Alchip
and also remaining strong capex expansion from its major CSP customers, we lift

our earnings projection for Alchip by 11%/15%/32% for 2026/2027 /2028. We
maintain Buy on Alchip with higher target price NT$6,800 (from NT$6,200), based
on 35x of our 2027 EPS estimate. We lower our target PER from 37x to 35x to
reflect recent market uncertainties.

### Page 7

Alchip (3661.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
7
Alchip
Company description
Alchip is a leading provider of silicon design and manufacturing service,
specializing in ASIC (Application-Specific Integrated Circuit) design,
development, and production. Founded in 2003, the company has >1,000
employees and operates in Taipei, Shanghai, Japan, Korea, and the US. Alchip
serves a wide range of industries, including automotive, industrials, consumer
electronics, and computing.

Investment strategy
We rate Alchip as Buy. We expect Alchip to deliver a strong snapback in
2026/27 with its N3 project ramping up. Moreover, we are also positive over
the long-term growth opportunity from its AI ASIC projects with its major CSP
client. Alchip remains one of the few design service providers within TSMC’s
3DFabric alliance. In our view, if CSPs work more aggressively on their internal
design and become more competitive on product offerings, Alchip would be

well positioned as a back-end/turnkey partner.
Valuation
Our target price for Alchip of NT$6,800 is based on 35x 2027E EPS. 35x
target PER is largely in line with its historical 3-year PER range, which we see
as justified on account of strong growth potential of its AI ASIC projects with
its key CSP client, with better project cycle and strong growth momentum
resuming next year. Our target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 11x/8x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected NRE

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

M
Update
King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Preliminary Profit in July
Announced
King Slide announced its preliminary profit for July, per TWSE regulation:
• Revenue was NT$6,407mn (+44% MoM/+356% YoY), 33% of our estimate at
NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348% YoY) and 40% of the consensus at NT
$15,883mn (+47% QoQ/+264% YoY) for 3Q26.
• Pretax income was NT$5,985mn (+321% YoY; pretax margin at 93.4%), 37%
of our pretax income estimate at NT$16,377mn (margin at 83.7%) and 45% of

consensus at NT$13,170mn (margin at 82.9%) for 3Q26.
• Net income was NT$4,787mn (+321% YoY; EPS NT$50.24; NPM 74.7%), 37%
of our estimated net income at NT$12,938mn (EPS NT$135.76; NPM 66.2%)
and 46% of consensus at NT$10,460mn (EPS NT$108.72; NPM 65.9%) for
3Q26.
Our view:
• We see upside to our estimates and consensus for 3Q26 earnings.
• There should be some regular interest income and one-off FX gains during
the month, but even with that, our back-of-the-envelop calculation points to

over 85% of operating margin in July vs. 82.1% in 2Q26. We believe this
should strengthen the market's conviction about the sustainability of its
margins in 2H26.
• We maintain our view that King Slide's competitiveness of the
comprehensive patent portfolio and strong execution is entrenched and
accentuated amid the fast generation migration in AI applications.
• Stay OW with PT of NT$15,000 (30x 2027 P/E)
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

水冷滲透率越來越高，預期 2027
年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU

水冷滲透率越來越高，預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU 
展望 3Q26，七月營收 185.9 億元，+5.5%MOM，+57.39%YOY，預期八月及
九月營收續強，3Q26 將開始出貨少量 VR200 及 ASIC 水冷產品，預期 4Q26
放量，故 4Q26 營 收 動 能 亦 為 雙 位 數QOQ 增長，元富預估 3Q26 營收
+10~15%QOQ，4Q26 營收+10~15%QOQ，2027 年隨 ASIC 機種放量，推估
奇鋐於 ASIC 機種出貨品項將多於 GPU 客戶，故以奇鋐出 ASIC 單櫃散熱產值
來看亦將優於 GPU 客戶，預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將首度超過 GPU，成長動能
可期。獲利部分，隨產品組合優化及自動化程度提升，預期2H26 毛利率將優於
2Q26，2027 年毛利率亦優於 2026 年。

針對擴產計劃來看，由於目前奇鋐舊廠產能均為滿載，越南新廠產能逐步爬升
稼動率，2027 年因應客戶訂單需求將擴增水冷各產品線產能約 +50%，2026 年
奇鋐資本支出為 150 億元，2027 年資本支出將高於 2026 年，長線成長動能可
期。關於雙相式液冷，公司認為散熱不是每個案子都用最新技術，將視案件實
際需求為主，以下一世代機種來看，TDP 權衡結果仍採用單相式為主，將持續
跟客戶討論雙相式應用。

維持買進，目標價 4250 元

維持買進評等，目標價 4250 元 
水冷滲透率持續提升，預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU，營收及毛利率方
向均樂觀，故維持買進，預估 2026 年 EPS 102.39 元，2027 年 EPS 157.75
元，目標價 4250 元。

風險提示 
出貨不如預期，ASP 不如預期。

2026/08/14 投資速報

嘉澤 (3533.TT)

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com

嘉澤 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 17.68 元，低於研究部原估 21.28 元。
受到金、銅、塑膠等原物料價格大幅上漲影響，且嘉澤為維持市佔
自行吸收成本，加上記憶體缺料壓抑伺服器出貨、新平台放量遞延
至 2027 年，短期獲利持續受到壓抑。研究部因此下修嘉澤 2026、
2027 年稅後 EPS 至 80.59 元（+15.3% YoY，原估 92.36 元）及 
110.19 元（+36.7% YoY，原估 119.88 元）。儘管現價對應 2027 
年本益比約 18 倍，惟考量新平台放量及毛利率修復需待 1H27，
短期營運動能明顯轉弱，故將投資評等由買進調降至中立，目標價 
2,000 元（2027 年 PER 18 倍）。

重點摘要： 
1. 高價原物料成本全面反映，2Q26 獲利大幅低於預期 
嘉澤 2Q26 營收 95.69 億元（+2.6% QoQ、+14.8% YoY），低於原
估 101.35 億元，主因記憶體等零組件缺料壓抑伺服器出貨，消費
性電子需求亦持續疲弱。受到低價原物料庫存於 1Q26 消耗完畢，
金、銅、塑膠等原物料成本全面反映，使原物料占銷貨成本比重由
過往 45-50% 升至 65%，加上新產品仍處學習曲線前期影響，導致
毛利率大幅低於預期，2Q26 毛利率僅 45.47%（-4.08ppt QoQ 、-
5.53ppt YoY） ，低於原估 50.1%。費用亦高於預期，因認列一次性
員工認股費用 2.79 億元，2Q26 營業費用達 22.48 億元（費用率 
23.49%），高於原估的 21.28 億元（原估費用率21.0%）。毛利率
與 費用 率 雙 雙 不 如預 期 下 ， 營益 率 僅 21.97% ，大 幅 低於 原 估 
29.1%。單季稅後純益 19.9 億元（-17.0% QoQ、+165.6% YoY），
EPS 17.68 元，低於原估 21.28 元。

圖一、2024~2027F 嘉澤產品營收比重

資料來源：嘉澤，統一投顧整理

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 缺料壓抑出貨、毛利率持平谷底，下修 3Q26 獲利預估 
嘉澤 7 月營收 33.69 億元（+8.9% MoM 、+18.6% YoY），對原估 
3Q26 營收達成率僅 30.2%。伺服器 ODM 月產量受記憶體缺料影
響，由 2Q26 的 1.49KK 降至 7、8 月的 1.32KK。毛利率方面，
金、銅、塑膠等原物料成本維持高檔，且為避免市佔流失，未向客
戶轉嫁成本，僅能仰賴新品良率改善與內部降本吸收壓力，毛利率
將持續承壓，預期 3Q26 毛利率僅較 2Q26 小幅回升。研究部下修 
3Q26 營收預估由 111.47 億元至 102.35 億元 （+7.0% QoQ、+21.5% 
YoY），毛利率由原估 50.9% 下修至 45.5%，營益率由原估 29.9% 
下修至 26.5%，稅後 EPS 由原估 23.96 元下修至 19.64 元
（+11.1% QoQ、-4.6% YoY）。

3. 成本維持高檔，2026 年獲利下修 13% 
全年度來看，桌機受 DIY 客戶轉回舊平台影響全年衰退近四成，
筆電受缺料壓抑年減約 14%；伺服器受惠通用型需求強勁與產品組
合升級全年成長約 70%，車用亦維持 15% 成長，惟原物料占銷貨
成本比重升至 65% 的結構下 ， 產品組合優化不足以抵銷成本上升 ，
研究部因此下修嘉澤 2026 年獲利 13%。預估 2026 年合併營收 
400.07 億元（+18.4% YoY，原估 422.42 億元），毛利率 46.7%
（-4.6ppt YoY，原估 50.7%），2026 年稅後 EPS 80.59 元（原
估 92.36 元）。

4. 新平台世代升級與 AI 新應用，成本轉嫁可期 
展望未來 ，Intel Birch Stream 滲透率持續拉升 （2Q26 已占 Intel 
平台約 40%），Oak Stream 與 AMD Venice 新平台將自 4Q26 起
陸續放量 ，SP7 腳位數由 SP5 的 6,096 pins 提升至 7,536 pins，
並同步升級至 PCIe Gen6 ，帶動 Socket ASP 至少提升 15%。此
外，新平台產品依現行原物料價格重新報價，成本得以完全轉嫁，
毛利率可望隨新平台出貨比重提升自 1H27 起明顯回升。AI 新應
用亦逐步展開，QD 液冷快接頭已於 7 月量產並切入一家 CSP 與
一家平台客戶 ，2H26 目標占營收 2-3%；SOCAMM 打入 NVIDIA Vera 
Rubin 且稼動率逾九成；NPO Socket 預計 2027 年量產，中長期
成長動能明確。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

5. 待新平台放量，2027 年獲利重回高成長 
隨新一世代伺服器於 2027 年放量、新平台重新報價與 AI 新產品
貢獻擴大，2027 年獲利可望重回高成長。惟新一世代伺服器放量
時間遞延，出貨貢獻較原估延後，研究部下修 2027 年合併營收至 
496.32 億元 （+24.1% YoY，原估 504.79 億元） 。另外，毛利率則
因重新報價僅適用新平台，加上 QD、線材等低毛利新產品放量將
稀釋毛利率，因此下修毛利率至 49.1%（原估 53.4%）。稅後 EPS 
下修至 110.19 元（原估 119.88 元）。

圖二、伺服器 CPU 產品 Roadmap

短期毛利率承壓

永豐觀點

 投資評價與建議

年 EPS 6.45 元，2027 年 EPS 8.95 元，目標價 107 元(12X 2027 EPS)。

2

中磊為電信網通設備專業製造廠：1H26 各產品營收占比分別為 Broadband CPE 
60％，IoT & Infra 21％，Enterprise 19％。主要客戶為歐美龍頭電信運營商與有
線頻道業者及系統整合商，中磊的主要競爭力在於軟韌體與硬體協同開發設計能
力、客製化服務能力，主要競爭對手為國內網通業者啟碁 (6285 TT) 與智易(3596 
TT)等。 
2Q26 財報不如預期：中磊 1Q26 EPS 1.58 元，不如預期的 1.81 元，主因毛利率
表現受記憶體成本漲價而不如預期 ， 公司表示2Q26 有先替客戶吸收一些成本 ， 營
收+69%YoY 裡面其中有+20-30%YoY 是來自於記憶體漲價，一些 eMMC、DDR
原本占成本 10%左右 ， 現在在部分設備成本佔比來到50%， 也導致毛利率被稀釋 。 
3Q26 營收維持續增的看法：中磊先前展望已提到 3Q26 營收將繼續季增，但毛利
率受成本增加影響預計增幅相對有限 ， 研究部考量2Q26 財報毛利率不如預期 ， 短
期記憶體缺料漲價也無解 ， 故下調公司毛利率預估 ， 整體預估3Q26 營收為 210.00
億元(+1.9%QoQ，+42.4%YoY)， 毛利率12.4%， 營業利益率3.5%，稅後淨利 5.18
億元(+9.4%QoQ，+74.7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 1.73 元。 
仍面臨記憶體缺料挑戰：公司表示記憶體缺料到 2027 年可能更嚴重，為了維持客
戶長期關係也無法硬調漲產品價格， 故研究部認為毛利率短期內仍會承壓， 高毛利
率產品 DAA 遞延到 2027 年才明顯放量，故 2027 年毛利率較有改善空間，整體

年，初期產品主要以主機板、介面卡、工控

張智皓 chihhao.chang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

飛捷 (6206 TT) 
支付結合 POS 為新動能，軟體成長如預期

永豐觀點 
2Q26 獲利優於預期，3Q 訂單掌握度佳，全年營運展望樂觀。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議 ：在新客戶堆疊及軟體營收持續成長下，飛捷營運將迎來開花結
果，2025 年推出的 Payment POS (POS+支付)，1H26 產品滲透率已達 10%，預
估全年將達 20%，有利於飛捷在營收及獲利狀態。此外，軟體佔營收比重將持續
提高並因軟體訂閱費疊加改善獲利結構，預計三年內將開始獲利。評價方面，以
2026 年預估稅後 EPS 11.32 元計算，目前約為 14 倍本益比，過去五年平均本益
比約為 16 倍 ， 考量營運能見度佳 ， 產品及客戶組合優化 ，故維持買進的投資建議 ，
給予 17 倍本益比，目標價 193 元。

### Page 2

全球 POS 機領導廠商：飛捷成立於 1984 年，初期產品主要以主機板、介面卡、
工控卡等產品為主，1999 年研發生產分離式端點銷售系統(POS system)，業務漸
以 POS、Kiosk 為主。2002 年掛牌上櫃，2004 年上市，2016 年併購英國 Box 
100%股權，主要提供POS、KIOSK、數位看板等服務、陸續成立華捷智能、Berry 
AI、Angible、inefi 等公司。飛捷產能位於台灣林口地區，總部位於台北，全球共
有英國、美國、香港、上海等四個辦公室，全球約 700 名員工。飛捷除大中華(中
國、台灣、香港)以自有品牌行銷外，其他地區均為 ODM 產品，客戶為擁有自有
品牌之經銷商或 SI，終端應用包含餐飲、零售、醫療、工廠自動化，前兩大客戶為
美洲、歐洲經銷商。 
2Q26 獲利歷史新高：在終端市場需求強勁、營運槓桿效益顯現、軟體虧損逐步收
窄下，飛捷 2Q26 營收 18.82 億元(+24.6%QoQ，+38.3%YoY)，毛利率 45.4%(-
0.2ptsQoQ，+1.5pts YoY)， 在費用控管得宜下 ， 營業淨利5.81 億元(+37%QoQ，
+62.8%YoY)， 稅後淨利4.89 億元(+36.1%QoQ，+128.5%YoY)， 稅後EPS 為 3.41
元，優於預期的 2.88 元，為歷史新高。現金股利方面，飛捷將配發約 5.5 元現金
股利，配發率 80%，換算殖利率約為 3.5%(以 8/13 收盤價計算)。 
3Q26 訂單能見度佳，營運維持高檔：展望第三季，整體市場需求仍屬強勁，在手
訂單掌握度佳，預估營運仍將維持強勁。原物料方面，飛捷採取積極備料的政策，
第二季庫存較第一季增加約兩億元 ， 因應原物料成本不斷上漲 。 飛捷七月營收5.68
億元(-6.8%MoM，+38.2%YoY)， 達成率31.9%， 符合預期 。 研究員預估飛捷3Q26
營收 17.82 億元(-10.6%QoQ，+45.3%YoY)， 毛利率44.9%(-0.5ptsQoQ，+2pts 
YoY)，營業淨利 5.21 億元(-10.3%QoQ，+85.4%YoY)，稅後淨利 4.22 億元(-
13.6%QoQ，+88.8%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 2.95 元。 
Payment POS (POS+支付)及軟體比重提升，2026 年營運樂觀：展望 2026 年，
過往耕耘的新客戶已逐漸開花見效， 成長受惠於應用場域越加多元、 缺工自助化普
及、過往合作客戶發展逐漸成熟、軟體系統被大客戶採用。另一成長動能來自於
2025 年推出的 Payment POS (POS+支付)，1H26 產品滲透率已達 10%，預估全
年將達 20%，因產品單價較高，有利於飛捷在營收及獲利上的表現。在 Barry AI
方面，產品專注於幫助客戶流程安排、消費者點餐及排隊狀況紀錄，提升營運經營
效率，產品發展成熟後邁入收成期，主要客戶為北美餐飲業者，並持續將產品拓展
至各州 ， 目前Barry AI 滲透率仍僅低個位數 ， 整體潛在市場成長空間相當大 。Barry 
AI 財務上，2025 年虧損約七千萬，預期在滲透率提升下，2026 年營收將達翻倍

朋億 *(6613 TT)  Trading Buy

2Q26 毛利率低於預期 ， 近期營收呈現月減 ， 給Trading Buy
的投資建議。  
投資建議 ：朋億 *受惠於半導體、記憶體及先進封裝等客戶擴廠
需求，營運正向，但短期營收成長趨緩，給 Trading Buy 的投資
建議。  
朋億 *1Q26 在手訂單約 127 億元 ：日月光投控 (3711 TT) 06/25
公告，代子公司矽品向朋億取得廠務工程，金額約 5.08 億元，
加上半導體、記憶體客戶的相關訂單，累計至 1Q26 的在手訂單
逾百億元， YoY+81% 。  
朋億 *2Q26 獲利季增、年增 ：朋億 * 2Q26 營收 37.87 億元，
QoQ+61.71%/YoY+85.31% ；稅後純益 3.63 億元，
QoQ+21.52%/YoY+134.54% ；稅後 EPS 4.67 元。 2Q26 毛利率
23.98% ，低於群益預估的 25.50% 
朋億 *07/2026 營收月減 ：朋億 * (6613 TT) 公布 07/2026 營收為
12.93 億元， MoM-35.14% 、 YoY+64.32% ，累計營收為 74.21 億
元， YoY+49.04% 。  
朋億 *3Q26 營運審慎樂觀 ：目前半導體、記憶體與先進封裝客
戶建廠及擴產需求仍具良好能見度，可望挹注後續業務接單與
營運表現。  
朋億 *客戶擴廠積極 ：受惠 AI 晶片需求持續帶動國際半導體、
記憶體相關大廠加大資本支出投入新廠擴建及產能擴增計畫，
帶動主要客戶對於高潔淨度化學品供應與分裝系統設備訂單認
列、下單需求轉趨積極。

微殘留 1c.c. 針具下，將可多打 250 萬人，對國人疫苗的短缺帶來新的希望。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 302: Korea Equity Strategy_ Update on the de-leveraging process in Korea (2)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Korea Equity Strategy_ Update on the de-leveraging process in Korea (2).pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-13
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

J.P. Morgan Securities (Asia Pacific) Limited/ J.P.
Morgan Broking (Hong Kong) Limited
The Korea market has undergone an intense period of de-leveraging since mid-
June. The KOSPI index is now down almost -40% from the peak on 22 June in a
highly volatile move. What was triggered by routine fundamental concerns and
rotational flow was amplified by leveraged ETFs, and in recent days increasingly
has the hallmarks of hedge fund positioning unwind. Following our update last

week (where we estimated that leveraged ETF unwind was 75% through and equity
H/F de-leveraging >50% through), we now believe that leveraged ETF unwind
is complete and hedge funds are done with ~90% of de-leveraging (both to
acceptable levels). While it is likely that the price damage we have seen this week
has residual spillovers into further de-leveraging over the coming days, and that
many investors continue to exercise caution into the FOMC this week (high risk

of a rate hike) and hyperscaler earnings (high expectations), the positioning setup
in Korea now appears attractive on balance – and complements the cheap
valuations and earnings momentum.
• De-leveraging update: (1) Leveraged ETFs AUM has normalized:
Leveraged ETFs with Korea underlyings grew to $50bn in AUM by late June.
Relative to its market size, this was 4x larger than the US and led to very
pronounced volatility (and forced de-leveraging on down-days – which then

caused positioning unwinds from other investors). With the reversal of the
market, this cohort is now down to $17bn. In addition, the previously rapid
inflows into these products have stalled in recent days (not much dip buying).
As a result, the VKOSPI to VIX ratio has started to decline and will likely fall
further. (2) HF leverage has almost normalized: Swap capacity constraints
kept a lid on HF leverage buildup in Korea. Still, L/S ratios in the JPM Prime

book rose up to 5.7x. The process of normalization from there is now well
advanced. This ratio dropped to 3.2x by 27 Jul, and given the unwind in the
Price Momentum factor over 28 and 29 Jul, HF leverage has likely declined
further, and not very far from the top end of 2025’s range. (3) Retail leverage
through margins less of a risk: Margin balances were never particularly high
or witnessing rapid growth. Balances have moderated somewhat to ~$20bn

now. And, unlike leveraged ETFs that undergo a process of forced de-
leveraging on any spot price declines, margin lending comes with buffers and
discretion. Korean retail investors still have ample equity gains, cash balances,
higher incomes and overseas assets to tap into to absorb any margin calls should
they want to keep the position. (4) Record foreign outflows are slowing: LO
selling pressure has substantially eased as Korea (and particularly the memory

names) has underperformed, meaning that the two heavyweights are now only
6.5% and 4.5% weights in MSCI EM (vs 9.5% and 8.3% in late June).
• Diversification trades remain in play:  We continue to like: (1) “ wealth
effect” exposures (department stores, cosmetics, travel, brokers, construction)
have a compelling tailwind; (2) Bio-pharma is a substantial laggard that
potentially benefits from globally improving sentiment around the healthcare
sector; (3) Pref share discounts are near record wides – with the resultant

### Page 1

ab 10 August 2026
Global Research
APAC Tech Strategy
Which sectors within the AI Trade screen well
post the Sell-off?
Time to start re-visiting some of the hardest hit sectors within APAC Tech
Since late June, APAC tech stocks have broadly retrenched, with the MSCI ex Japan IT
index down 20% from June 22nd, largely in line with the semis SOX index down 16%.
This coming after nearly 3 months of a very sustained rally. Amongst the larger caps, the
correction has been particularly marked for SK Hynix (-51% from peak) and Samsung (-

37%). We believe technical factors played a large part: 1/ Retail ETF volumes in both
stocks, which peaked out around the same time (see Page 6), and have materially come
down since (a positive); 2/ over-crowding with many Asia Ex/GEM active long only equity
funds reaching 10% single stock limit – crowding factors are now less pronounced (Page
3). Fundamentally though, investors also grew more worried about the sustainability of
the AI cycle, with financing a growing concern (evidenced by rising credit spreads).

Sectors such as Memory, IC Substrates, MLCCs – which combine AI demand drivers and
(til then) growing supply shortages –  ended particularly exposed, followed by Semiscap.
All top 4 US hyperscalers have since reported, with our global Tech research team raising
2026E/2027E operating cash flow forecasts. From here, we expect concerns to start to
ease. We recommend investors to revisit several of the AI-exposed sub-sectors,
with our order of preference being Memory > MLCCs > Semiscap.

Why memory spending at hyperscalers (and how to control it) is so critical
Our revised forecasts for the top 12 hyperscalers capex stands at $975bn in 2026E
(+92% YoY) and $1,424bn in 2027E (+46% YoY), with still possible upside mainly
driven by memory spending (directly or indirectly). Importantly, we have slightly reduced
our estimate for hyperscalers memory spending to $899bn in 2027E from $356bn in
2026E, still a YoY increment of $543bn. This comes after continuing increases YTD,

which led Memory to be the biggest driver of capex hikes at hyperscalers. The reason for
this are LTAs between memory makers and hyperscalers, which help to control some of
the memory pricing upside into the upcycle, combined with a higher LTA coverage ratio
of total volumes – >50% of memory bits by YE vs. our 35% assumption prior – and
higher for hyperscalers. LTAs are making the memory upcycle more sustainable, a
positive for both hyperscalers as customers, and memory makers as they are ROE

accretive through-cycle (link).
HOLT screening of the 50 largest market caps within APAC Tech
Market-implied CFROI has fallen by nearly 100 bp since June, in line with the recent
pullback in tech. Even so, the sector’s fundamental outlook remains supported, with
CFROI forecast to increase by 240–570 bp from 2025 levels. Earnings sentiment also
remains positive, although the magnitude of CFROI revisions has moderated from the
April/May highs. Stocks that screen as cheap relative to history, have positive one-month

CFROI revisions and meet the HOLT operational quality peer-rank threshold include:
Accton, Advantest, Aspeed, GUC, Quanta, Samsung, TSMC and Wiwynn (all Buy).
Changes to APAC Tech Most / Least Preferred lists
We add to our Most Preferred list Accton, Advantest, and AEM whilst removing
Kelington. We also replace Sunny Optical with Desay in our Least Preferred list.
Most Preferred: Accton, Advantest, AEM, All Ring, ASE, Eugene Tech, Global
Unichip, Hansol Chem, Hon Hai, ISU Petasys, JCET Group, MediaTek, Murata, NAURA,

+886-2-8722 7335

APAC Tech Strategy UBS Research
Investor Positioning
Within APAC Tech top 50 market cap, SK Hynix, TSMC, Samsung, Kioxia, and Tokyo
Electron remain the most over-crowded. Still, those factors have receded for all 5 bar
Kioxia. Our discussions with investors also indicate more divergence in investors views
for Memory than in 2Q26.
Crowding scores are based on a proprietary combination of multiple data sets measured

at the individual stock level, capturing both long and short positions in a single, net score
for greater stability and directness. Scores range from approximately -30 (very short
crowded) to 30 (very long crowded), offering a more robust alternative to indirect or
incomplete crowding measures.

### Page 9

APAC Tech Strategy 10 August 2026 ab 9
APAC Tech Strategy UBS Research
APAC TECH Through the HOLT Lens
Driven by AI-related demand, CFROI forecasts for APAC tech have risen steadily since
early 2023, peaking at 12.3% in February this year. Since then, forecast CFROI has
declined by nearly 60 bp amid renewed geopolitical uncertainty in the Middle East, rising
energy prices and inflation pressures.
Market-implied CFROI has also fallen by nearly 100 bp since June, in line with the recent

pullback in tech stocks. Even so, the sector’s fundamental outlook remains supported,
with CFROI forecast to increase by 240–570 bp from 2025 levels. Earnings sentiment
also remains positive, although the magnitude of CFROI revisions has moderated from
the April/May highs.

### Page 12

APAC Tech Strategy 10 August 2026 ab 12
APAC Tech Strategy UBS Research
AI Cycle and hyperscalers CAPEX
UBS Global Tech Research capex forecasts are now better reflecting the significant
impact Memory has on it. We now forecast capex for the top 12 hyperscalers to reach
$975bn in 2026 (+92% YoY) and $1,424bn in 2027 (+46% YoY). For the top 9 – for
which we have OCF forecasts – we now forecast capex to reach $916bn in 2026 (+88%
YoY) and $1,358bn in 2027 (+48% YoY). On the positive side, we have increased our

OCF forecasts for the top 9 to $914bn in 2026 and $1,272bn in 2027, reducing the gap
between capex and OCF.

### Page 16

APAC Tech Strategy 10 August 2026 ab 16
APAC Tech Strategy UBS Research
PC & Smartphone DEmand
2Q26 ODM notebook shipment came in ahead our expectations with +4% QoQ, -8%
YoY to 30.4mn units as H126 saw strong pull-in ahead of component price hike
concern. However, our supply chain check suggests the momentum is likely to end in H1
with a widespread conservativeness on H2 demand. Our 2026 total ODM shipment is
therefore trimmed to -10% YoY (from LSD decline) reflecting continued memory

shortage along with CPU/passive component constraint, aligned with UBS global PC
forecast of -11% YoY.

June '26 YTD global smartphone unit sell-through now stands 551.4m units (-
7.8% YoY), with all regions in negative comps. With increasing memory costs
continuing to weigh on bill of material (BOM) costs of smartphones – particularly mid-
range/low-end devices – we continue to see downside to smartphone demand and
expect smartphone unit sell-in to decline by ~14% YoY in 2H26 and by 10% YoY for the
full-year.
